# 客户、供应商、银行账户与往来偏好

查找和维护往来单位、应收应付科目与会计属性、伙伴银行账户，以及开票交付、账单校验、收付偏好和信用限额。伙伴会计属性和偏好设置不是创建发票、执行支付或实际发送邮件；查询信用暴露可用于判断往来风险。

[回到总说明书](../../CLI_V4_MANUAL.md) · [新会话使用指南](../USAGE_GUIDE.md)

<a id="cap-bank-get"></a>

## bank.get — 获取银行主数据详情

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`bank_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, res.bank, res.country, res.country.state；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, res.bank:read, res.country:read, res.country.state:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/bank.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/bank.get.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read bank.get --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "bank_id": 1
  }
}
```

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| parameters | object | 必填 |  |  | {"additionalProperties":false,"required_in_object":["bank_id"],"resolved_ref":"#/$defs/parameters"} |
| parameters.bank_id | integer | 必填（所在对象出现时） |  |  | {"minimum":1} |

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_context_core_object_read_action
- `verify`：same_transaction_acl_result_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Focused tests cover the closed request and response schemas, registry metadata, CLI handler, validator, model, and audit routing.；引用：tests/unit/test_accounting_reference_read_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared live read-only smoke verifies the capability against both dedicated isolated database aliases without database changes.；引用：tests/integration/test_accounting_reference_read_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples are deferred until the capability library reaches target coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing is outside this capability batch.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-bank-list"></a>

## bank.list — 列出银行主数据

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`bank_list`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, res.bank, res.country, res.country.state；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, res.bank:read, res.country:read, res.country.state:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/bank.list.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/bank.list.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read bank.list --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {}
}
```

|---|---|---|---|---|---|

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_context_core_object_read_action
- `verify`：same_transaction_acl_result_cursor_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Focused tests cover the closed request and response schemas, registry metadata, CLI handler, validator, model, and audit routing.；引用：tests/unit/test_accounting_reference_read_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared live read-only smoke verifies the capability against both dedicated isolated database aliases without database changes.；引用：tests/integration/test_accounting_reference_read_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples are deferred until the capability library reaches target coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing is outside this capability batch.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-company-credit_policy-update"></a>

## company.credit_policy.update — 设置公司信用限额启用选项

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — Fixed current-company settings are implemented; native company write requires access-rights administration and reference ACLs. Ordinary accountant permission is not implied.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：ir.default, res.company；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：base.group_erp_manager；ACL：ir.default:read, res.company:read, res.company:write。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/company.credit_policy.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/company.credit_policy.update.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run company.credit_policy.update --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "company.credit_policy.update:1:812dd42c0b7ed6fd75aefb452883d21d" --confirm "company.credit_policy.update"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "changes": {
      "account_use_credit_limit": false
    }
  }
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：native_res_company_write_with_fixed_operation_fields_as_business_user
- `verify`：same_savepoint_configuration_reread_with_native_constraints
- `idempotency`：serial_current_target_setting_recheck_without_operation_store_or_concurrent_exactly_once
- `reverse`：restore_prior_settings_subject_to_native_constraints

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Closed operation-specific settings, native typed choices and null clearing, public CLI/schemas/confirmation, deterministic keys and contextual company/result binding. Real ORM behavior belongs to the shared integration workflow.；引用：tests/unit/test_company_processing_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；One shared rollback-only public CLI/real-ORM workflow passed both isolated aliases as uid 5/su=False/company 1: eight new IDs and two reused journal-entry setup IDs; fourteen setting replays plus one posting replay per alias. Ordinary business-user settings read succeeds and native company write is denied before a transaction-local ERP-access group grant. All seven fixed native company writes persist and serial target-state replay is verified. Native April31 fiscal-end validation and existing-accounting price-method denial return the actual ValidationError/business_rule_error and restore all partial changes; February29 is natively accepted. Current-company reads, relation null clearing, all three quick-edit choices and disabled null mode are checked. All seven relation fields reject foreign and missing references; exchange journal selection rejects non-general sale journals. Posted entry identities/accounts/amounts/residuals/taxes, the other company and non-target lock/audit/chart/currency/prefix configuration are unchanged. Actual product-category default values match native company accounts after company.write; fresh cursors prove existing company settings, all ir.default rows, exact groups and synthetic objects roll back, including before rethrow on failure. The test loads the actual immutable CLI registry once per worker but retains real request/response validation and native ACLs. No concurrent exactly-once, child-company native scenario, real invoice rendering/delivery, automatic bill posting or credit-limit amount mutation claim. No business DB, installed addon/source or service/configuration changes; no ERP grant persisted.；引用：tests/integration/test_company_processing_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_local_odoo_readonly_composite
- `verify`：same_transaction_result_and_v1_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the strict request, cursor, bridge and fixed ORM contracts.；引用：tests/unit/test_partner_accounting.py, tests/unit/test_partner_bridge.py, tests/unit/test_partner_runtime.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；Live tests verify the fixed partner search across both synthetic databases and configured companies.；引用：tests/integration/test_partner_accounting_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-partner-accounting-update"></a>

## partner.accounting.update — 更新伙伴会计属性

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is registered; availability depends on runtime configuration and the configured user satisfying both the accounting-user group check and res.partner write access. Temporary live-test grants do not establish default user permission.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner, account.account, account.fiscal.position, account.payment.term；向导：无。
- 必需模块：base, account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user, base.group_partner_manager；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read, res.partner:write, account.account:read, account.fiscal.position:read, account.payment.term:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.accounting.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.accounting.update.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run partner.accounting.update --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "partner.accounting.update:1:e11d489e5c84de8f12c8ecafd156c965" --confirm "partner.accounting.update"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "partner_id": 1,
    "changes": {
      "property_account_receivable_id": 1
    }
  }
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_partner_accounting_update_as_configured_business_user
- `verify`：post_write_same_transaction_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：deterministic_partner_target_and_changes_digest32_request_key_with_current_target_state_recheck_without_operation_store_and_old_request_may_act_again_after_intermediate_changes
- `reverse`：partner.accounting.update_with_prior_values

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed accounting-property whitelist and explicit-null clears, child-partner rejection, fixed target-state recheck and immediate replay, declared accounting-user/partner-manager and ACL metadata, fail-closed schemas/results, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_partner_master_data.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_cli.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded transactional smoke passed the full 11-capability chain in both isolated databases, verifying search/get visibility, private-marker stripping on get, first execution plus immediate replay for all nine writes, and rollback of partner/bank fixtures and the temporary partner-manager grant; the grant is test-only and does not establish default user permission.；引用：tests/integration/test_partner_master_data_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-partner-archive"></a>

## partner.archive — 归档伙伴

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is registered; availability depends on runtime configuration and the configured user actually holding partner-management access. Temporary live-test grants do not establish default user permission.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner；向导：无。
- 必需模块：base, account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：base.group_partner_manager；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read, res.partner:write。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.archive.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.archive.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run partner.archive --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "partner.archive:1" --confirm "partner.archive"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "partner_id": 1
  }
}
```

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| parameters | object | 必填 |  |  | {"additionalProperties":false,"required_in_object":["partner_id"]} |
| parameters.partner_id | integer | 必填（所在对象出现时） |  | 合作伙伴ID | {"minimum":1} |

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_partner_bank_account_create_as_configured_business_user
- `verify`：post_write_same_transaction_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：deterministic_partner_and_trimmed_raw_account_number_digest32_request_key_with_current_partner_account_state_recheck_without_operation_store_and_old_request_may_act_again_after_intermediate_changes
- `reverse`：partner.bank_account.archive

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed bank-account contracts, owner/company isolation, reserved-marker rejection, fixed create/update/lifecycle state rechecks and immediate replay, declared ACL/group metadata, fail-closed schemas/results, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_partner_master_data.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_cli.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded transactional smoke passed the full 11-capability chain in both isolated databases, verifying search/get visibility, private-marker stripping on get, first execution plus immediate replay for all nine writes, and rollback of partner/bank fixtures and the temporary partner-manager grant; the grant is test-only and does not establish default user permission.；引用：tests/integration/test_partner_master_data_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-partner-bank_account-get"></a>

## partner.bank_account.get — 获取合作伙伴银行账户详情

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`partner_bank_account_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`payments`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner.bank, res.partner, res.bank, res.currency, account.journal；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner.bank:read, res.partner:read, res.bank:read, res.currency:read, account.journal:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.bank_account.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.bank_account.get.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read partner.bank_account.get --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "partner_bank_id": 1
  }
}
```

## partner.bank_account.restore — 恢复伙伴银行账户

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is registered; availability depends on runtime configuration and the configured user actually holding partner-management access. Temporary live-test grants do not establish default user permission.
- 内部domain：`payments`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner, res.partner.bank；向导：无。
- 必需模块：base, account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：base.group_partner_manager；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read, res.partner.bank:read, res.partner.bank:write。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.bank_account.restore.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.bank_account.restore.response.schema.json`。

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run partner.bank_account.restore --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "partner.bank_account.restore:1" --confirm "partner.bank_account.restore"
```

## partner.bank_account.search — 搜索合作伙伴银行账户

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`partner_bank_account_search`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`payments`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner.bank, res.partner, res.bank, res.currency, account.journal；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner.bank:read, res.partner:read, res.bank:read, res.currency:read, account.journal:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.bank_account.search.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.bank_account.search.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read partner.bank_account.search --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {}
}
```

### 请求参数（公共context与信封见总指南）

| 字段路径 | 类型 | 必填性/作用域 | 分支 | 含义 | 约束/默认值 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| parameters | object | 必填 |  |  | {"additionalProperties":false,"resolved_ref":"#/$defs/parameters"} |
| parameters.partner_id | integer/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 合作伙伴ID | {"default":null,"minimum":1} |

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed request and response, fixed bridge action, company scope, ACL gates, ORM normalization, pagination where applicable, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_core_object_reads.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_bridge.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_read_cli.py, tests/unit/test_capability_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared live read-only smoke verifies the capability against both dedicated isolated database aliases as the ordinary accounting user.；引用：tests/integration/test_payment_reconciliation_read_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-partner-bank_account-update"></a>

## partner.bank_account.update — 更新伙伴银行账户

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is registered; availability depends on runtime configuration and the configured user actually holding partner-management access. Temporary live-test grants do not establish default user permission.
- 内部domain：`payments`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner, res.partner.bank, res.bank, res.currency；向导：无。
- 必需模块：base, account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：base.group_partner_manager；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read, res.partner.bank:read, res.partner.bank:write, res.bank:read, res.currency:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.bank_account.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.bank_account.update.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run partner.bank_account.update --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "partner.bank_account.update:1:8b06605be56069af33620f88604007b3" --confirm "partner.bank_account.update"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "partner_bank_id": 1,
    "changes": {
      "account_number": "1"
    }
  }
}
```

### 请求参数（公共context与信封见总指南）

| 字段路径 | 类型 | 必填性/作用域 | 分支 | 含义 | 约束/默认值 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| parameters.changes.account_number | string | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  |  | {"maxLength":128,"minLength":1,"pattern":"^(?![\\s\\S]*\\[ODACV4:)(?:\\S&#124;\\S[\\s\\S]*\\S)$(?![\\s\\S])","resolved_ref":"#/$defs/markerSafeText128"} |
| parameters.changes.account_holder_name | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  |  | {"maxLength":256,"minLength":1,"pattern":"^(?![\\s\\S]*\\[ODACV4:)(?:\\S&#124;\\S[\\s\\S]*\\S)$(?![\\s\\S])","resolved_ref":"#/$defs/nullableMarkerSafeText256"} |
| parameters.changes.bank_id | integer/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  |  | {"minimum":1} |
| parameters.changes.currency_id | integer/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 币种ID | {"minimum":1} |

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_partner_bank_account_update_as_configured_business_user
- `verify`：post_write_same_transaction_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：deterministic_partner_bank_target_and_changes_digest32_request_key_with_current_target_state_recheck_without_operation_store_and_old_request_may_act_again_after_intermediate_changes
- `reverse`：partner.bank_account.update_with_prior_values

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed bank-account contracts, owner/company isolation, reserved-marker rejection, fixed create/update/lifecycle state rechecks and immediate replay, declared ACL/group metadata, fail-closed schemas/results, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_partner_master_data.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_cli.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded transactional smoke passed the full 11-capability chain in both isolated databases, verifying search/get visibility, private-marker stripping on get, first execution plus immediate replay for all nine writes, and rollback of partner/bank fixtures and the temporary partner-manager grant; the grant is test-only and does not establish default user permission.；引用：tests/integration/test_partner_master_data_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-partner-bill_validation_preferences-get"></a>

## partner.bill_validation_preferences.get — 读取供应商账单校验偏好

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`partner_bill_validation_preferences_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — Requires an explicit database/company/user runtime and native permissions; credit-limit configuration does not enable company credit checking or create a custom blocking policy.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.bill_validation_preferences.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.bill_validation_preferences.get.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read partner.bill_validation_preferences.get --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "partner_id": 1
  }
}
```

<a id="cap-partner-bill_validation_preferences-update"></a>

## partner.bill_validation_preferences.update — 修改供应商账单校验偏好

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`shared_partner_global_preference` — PDF-template and auto-post preferences are native non-company-dependent contact fields; changing them on a shared partner affects that partner across companies. Company-dependent fields remain scoped. No external delivery or bill posting is executed by this command.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：res.partner:read, res.partner:write。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.bill_validation_preferences.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.bill_validation_preferences.update.response.schema.json`。

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run partner.bill_validation_preferences.update --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "partner.bill_validation_preferences.update:1:0c54f02232e4d4fc579e806795cf2d56" --confirm "partner.bill_validation_preferences.update"
```

<a id="cap-partner-create"></a>

## partner.create — 创建伙伴

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`odoo_native_partner_ref_idempotency_field_unavailable` — Creation uses a visible [ODACV4:<digest>] marker in the partner ref for sequential replay detection. res.partner.ref has no database uniqueness constraint, so concurrent exactly-once creation is not guaranteed.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner, res.country.state, res.country；向导：无。
- 必需模块：base, account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：base.group_partner_manager；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read, res.partner:create, res.country.state:read, res.country:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.create.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.create.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run partner.create --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "partner.create:03086f200db2c260befb728c7caffc38" --confirm "partner.create"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
  "schema_version": "v1",

    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_partner_create_as_configured_business_user
- `verify`：post_write_same_transaction_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：visible_odoo_partner_ref_marker_from_full_normalized_parameters_digest32_for_sequential_replay_without_database_uniqueness_or_concurrent_exactly_once_guarantee
- `reverse`：partner.archive

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed normalized create contract, optional-field normalization, reserved-marker rejection, deterministic full-parameter key, fixed company-scoped creation and visible-ref immediate replay, declared ACL/group metadata, fail-closed schemas/results, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_partner_master_data.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_cli.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded transactional smoke passed the full 11-capability chain in both isolated databases, verifying search/get visibility, private-marker stripping on get, first execution plus immediate replay for all nine writes, and rollback of partner/bank fixtures and the temporary partner-manager grant; the grant is test-only and does not establish default user permission.；引用：tests/integration/test_partner_master_data_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-partner-credit_exposure-inspect"></a>

## partner.credit_exposure.inspect — 检查往来单位信用暴露

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`partner_credit_exposure_inspect`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed read handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`receivables_payables`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner, res.currency, account.move, account.move.line, account.account, account.invoice.report；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read, res.currency:read, account.move:read, account.move.line:read, account.account:read, account.invoice.report:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.credit_exposure.inspect.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.credit_exposure.inspect.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read partner.credit_exposure.inspect --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "partner_id": 1
  }
}
```

## partner.credit_limit.reset — 恢复客户公司默认信用额度

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — Requires an explicit database/company/user runtime and native permissions; credit-limit configuration does not enable company credit checking or create a custom blocking policy.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：res.partner:read, res.partner:write。

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。

- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner；向导：无。

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_native_partner_preference_patch_or_credit_inverse
- `verify`：same_transaction_native_preference_payload_recheck
- `idempotency`：native_current_payload_recheck
- `reverse`：restore_previous_preferences_with_native_acl

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Shared fixed contracts, monetary normalization, native credit fallback/inverse and batch framework tests.；引用：tests/unit/test_partner_preferences_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The guarded shared smoke passed both isolated aliases through the public CLI as uid 5 with su=False: all eight new capabilities, five immediate replays, native nonzero company-credit fallback restoration, invoice EDI clearing, payment-direction and company denial, company-dependent preference isolation, truthful shared global preferences, and fresh-cursor business-data, temporary-group and company-default rollback verification. No email/EDI delivery or bill posting was executed.；引用：tests/integration/test_partner_preferences_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples follow capability coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing follows capability coverage.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-partner-get"></a>

## partner.get — 获取伙伴详情

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`partner_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is registered; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner, res.country.state, res.country；向导：无。
- 必需模块：base, account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read, res.country.state:read, res.country:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.get.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read partner.get --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "partner_id": 1
  }
}
```

<a id="cap-partner-invoice_delivery_preferences-get"></a>

## partner.invoice_delivery_preferences.get — 读取客户发票交付偏好

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`partner_invoice_delivery_preferences_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — Requires an explicit database/company/user runtime and native permissions; credit-limit configuration does not enable company credit checking or create a custom blocking policy.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner, account.move, ir.actions.report；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read, account.move:read, ir.actions.report:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.invoice_delivery_preferences.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.invoice_delivery_preferences.get.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read partner.invoice_delivery_preferences.get --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "partner_id": 1
  }
}
```

<a id="cap-partner-invoice_delivery_preferences-update"></a>

## partner.invoice_delivery_preferences.update — 修改客户发票交付偏好

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`shared_partner_global_preference` — PDF-template and auto-post preferences are native non-company-dependent contact fields; changing them on a shared partner affects that partner across companies. Company-dependent fields remain scoped. No external delivery or bill posting is executed by this command.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：account.move, ir.actions.report, res.company, res.partner；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：account.move:read, ir.actions.report:read, res.partner:read, res.partner:write。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.invoice_delivery_preferences.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.invoice_delivery_preferences.update.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run partner.invoice_delivery_preferences.update --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "partner.invoice_delivery_preferences.update:1:45b8e2a61cb679d17a285732b9fdbe47" --confirm "partner.invoice_delivery_preferences.update"
```

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_native_partner_preference_patch_or_credit_inverse
- `verify`：same_transaction_native_preference_payload_recheck
- `idempotency`：native_current_payload_recheck
- `reverse`：restore_previous_preferences_with_native_acl

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Shared fixed contracts, monetary normalization, native credit fallback/inverse and batch framework tests.；引用：tests/unit/test_partner_preferences_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The guarded shared smoke passed both isolated aliases through the public CLI as uid 5 with su=False: all eight new capabilities, five immediate replays, native nonzero company-credit fallback restoration, invoice EDI clearing, payment-direction and company denial, company-dependent preference isolation, truthful shared global preferences, and fresh-cursor business-data, temporary-group and company-default rollback verification. No email/EDI delivery or bill posting was executed.；引用：tests/integration/test_partner_preferences_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples follow capability coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing follows capability coverage.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-partner-payment_preferences-get"></a>

## partner.payment_preferences.get — 读取客户供应商付款偏好

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`partner_payment_preferences_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — Requires an explicit database/company/user runtime and native permissions; credit-limit configuration does not enable company credit checking or create a custom blocking policy.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner, account.payment.method.line, account.journal；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read, account.payment.method.line:read, account.journal:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.payment_preferences.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.payment_preferences.get.response.schema.json`。

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read partner.payment_preferences.get --request "@request.json"
```

## partner.payment_preferences.update — 修改客户供应商付款偏好

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — Requires an explicit database/company/user runtime and native permissions; credit-limit configuration does not enable company credit checking or create a custom blocking policy.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：account.journal, account.payment.method.line, res.company, res.partner；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：account.journal:read, account.payment.method.line:read, res.partner:read, res.partner:write。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.payment_preferences.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.payment_preferences.update.response.schema.json`。

## partner.restore — 恢复伙伴

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is registered; availability depends on runtime configuration and the configured user actually holding partner-management access. Temporary live-test grants do not establish default user permission.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner；向导：无。
- 必需模块：base, account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：base.group_partner_manager；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read, res.partner:write。

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_partner_restore_as_configured_business_user
- `verify`：post_write_same_transaction_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：deterministic_partner_target_request_key_with_current_restored_state_recheck_without_operation_store_and_old_request_may_act_again_after_intermediate_changes
- `reverse`：partner.archive

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed partner mutation contracts, explicit-null handling where applicable, fixed company-scoped target/state rechecks and immediate replay, declared ACL/group metadata, fail-closed schemas/results, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_partner_master_data.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_cli.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded transactional smoke passed the full 11-capability chain in both isolated databases, verifying search/get visibility, private-marker stripping on get, first execution plus immediate replay for all nine writes, and rollback of partner/bank fixtures and the temporary partner-manager grant; the grant is test-only and does not establish default user permission.；引用：tests/integration/test_partner_master_data_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-partner-search"></a>

## partner.search — 搜索伙伴

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`partner_search`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is registered; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner, res.country.state, res.country；向导：无。
- 必需模块：base, account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read, res.country.state:read, res.country:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.search.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.search.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read partner.search --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {}
}
```

### 请求参数（公共context与信封见总指南）

| 字段路径 | 类型 | 必填性/作用域 | 分支 | 含义 | 约束/默认值 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| parameters | object | 必填 |  |  | {"additionalProperties":false} |
| parameters.query | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 搜索文本 | {"default":null,"maxLength":200,"minLength":1,"pattern":"^(?:\\S&#124;\\S[\\s\\S]*\\S)$(?![\\s\\S])"} |
| parameters.active | boolean/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  |  | {"default":null} |
| parameters.company_type | 未限定 | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  |  | {"default":null,"enum":["person","company",null]} |
| parameters.customer | boolean/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  |  | {"default":null} |
| parameters.supplier | boolean/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  |  | {"default":null} |
| parameters.limit | integer | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 每页数量 | {"default":100,"maximum":1000,"minimum":1} |
| parameters.cursor | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 不透明分页游标；新查询先省略，后续原样使用返回值 | {"default":null,"maxLength":4096,"minLength":1} |

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_core_object_read_action
- `verify`：same_transaction_acl_result_cursor_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the exact search filters, normalized defaults, page and fail-closed result contracts, fixed company-scoped runtime normalization with private-marker stripping, declared ACL/group metadata, registry schemas, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_partner_master_data.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_cli.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded transactional smoke passed the full 11-capability chain in both isolated databases, verifying search/get visibility, private-marker stripping on get, first execution plus immediate replay for all nine writes, and rollback of partner/bank fixtures and the temporary partner-manager grant; the grant is test-only and does not establish default user permission.；引用：tests/integration/test_partner_master_data_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-partner-update"></a>

## partner.update — 更新伙伴

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is registered; availability depends on runtime configuration and the configured user actually holding partner-management access. Temporary live-test grants do not establish default user permission.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, res.partner, res.country.state, res.country；向导：无。
- 必需模块：base, account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：base.group_partner_manager；ACL：res.company:read, res.partner:read, res.partner:write, res.country.state:read, res.country:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/partner.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/partner.update.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run partner.update --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "partner.update:1:663c40c5fc625d44d00a597ae77ce292" --confirm "partner.update"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "partner_id": 1,
    "changes": {
      "name": "Example"
    }
  }
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_partner_update_as_configured_business_user
- `verify`：post_write_same_transaction_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：deterministic_partner_target_and_changes_digest32_request_key_with_current_target_state_recheck_without_operation_store_and_old_request_may_act_again_after_intermediate_changes
- `reverse`：partner.update_with_prior_values

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed partner mutation contracts, explicit-null handling where applicable, fixed company-scoped target/state rechecks and immediate replay, declared ACL/group metadata, fail-closed schemas/results, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_partner_master_data.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_cli.py, tests/unit/test_partner_master_data_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded transactional smoke passed the full 11-capability chain in both isolated databases, verifying search/get visibility, private-marker stripping on get, first execution plus immediate replay for all nine writes, and rollback of partner/bank fixtures and the temporary partner-manager grant; the grant is test-only and does not establish default user permission.；引用：tests/integration/test_partner_master_data_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; execution evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。
